# Exact API changes — September 30, 2026 (UTC)

Comparison base: `b456fb0eab29f8e61795123bf7df5dc5b3a824c8`. All baseline endpoint definitions are byte-preserved. The first table quotes elaborated public types side by side, including new names. The second quotes changed definition bodies and source headers, so changes hidden behind a definition are visible. The complete machine-readable comparison is [public_api_changes.json](evidence/improvement/public_api_changes.json). Private helpers and generated fields are covered by the full declaration audit; they are not public headline APIs.

| `GC.LongTime.PersistentCuspExterior` | <code>(new declaration)</code> | <code>structure PersistentCuspExterior {P : OrientedThreeStage.{u}} {g : P.Metric}<br>    {F : GC.Interface.RawSurgery P g} {K : ℕ} (cores : PersistentHyperbolicCores F K) where<br>  truncation : (i : Fin cores.count) → HyperbolicTruncation (cores.model i)<br>  start : ℝ<br>  after_cores : cores.start ≤ start<br>  in_ball : ∀ i t, start ≤ t → range (truncation i).inclusion ⊆<br>    riemannianBallOf (cores.model i).metric<br>      (cores.model i).basepoint (cores.accuracy t)⁻¹</code> |
| `GC.LongTime.PersistentCuspExterior.region` | <code>(new declaration)</code> | <code>def PersistentCuspExterior.region {P : OrientedThreeStage.{u}} {g : P.Metric}<br>    {F : GC.Interface.RawSurgery P g} {K : ℕ} {cores : PersistentHyperbolicCores F K}<br>    (E : PersistentCuspExterior cores) (t : ℝ) : Set (postStage F.observation t).Carrier :=<br>  if ht : E.start ≤ t then<br>    (⋃ i, cores.map i t (E.after_cores.trans ht) &#x27;&#x27;<br>      ((E.truncation i).inclusion &#x27;&#x27; ((E.truncation i).core.interior : Set (E.truncation i).core.Carrier)))ᶜ<br>  else univ</code> |
| `GC.LongTime.PrescribedCuspMeridian` | <code>(new declaration)</code> | <code>structure PrescribedCuspMeridian {P : OrientedThreeStage.{u}} {g : P.Metric}<br>    {F : GC.Interface.RawSurgery P g} {K : ℕ} (cores : PersistentHyperbolicCores F K) where<br>  exterior : PersistentCuspExterior cores<br>  model : Fin cores.count<br>  port : Fin (exterior.truncation model).count<br>  loop : freeLoop Torus<br>  embedded : Topology.IsEmbedding loop<br>  smooth : ContMDiff 𝓘(ℝ, ℝ) torusModel ∞ (loopLift loop)<br>  geodesic : DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Riemannian.Geodesic.IsGeodesic<br>    ((exterior.truncation model).cusp port).torusMetric (loopLift loop)<br>  primitive : ∃ e : FundamentalGroup Torus (loop 0) ≃*<br>      Multiplicative ℤ × Multiplicative ℤ, e (loopDegreeClass loop 1) = (Multiplicative.ofAdd 1, 1)<br>  short : ∃ L : ℝ, 0 &lt; L ∧ L &lt; 1 ∧ ∀ s : ℝ,<br>    let v := mfderiv 𝓘(ℝ, ℝ) torusModel (loopLift loop) s 1;<br>    ((exterior.truncation model).cusp port).torusMetric.inner (loopLift loop s) v v ≤ L ^ 2<br>  transported : (t : ℝ) → exterior.start ≤ t → freeLoop (postStage F.observation t).Carrier<br>  prescribed : ∀ t (ht : exterior.start ≤ t) x,<br>    transported t ht x = cores.map model t (exterior.after_cores.trans ht)<br>      ((exterior.truncation model).cuspMap port (loop x, halfZero))<br>  spans : ∀ t (ht : exterior.start ≤ t), ∃ u : C(closedDisk, (postStage F.observation t).Carrier),<br>    isExteriorSpanningDisk (exterior.region t) (transported t ht) u</code> |
| `GC.LongTime.PrescribedCuspMeridian.loopAfter` | <code>(new declaration)</code> | <code>def PrescribedCuspMeridian.loopAfter {P : OrientedThreeStage.{u}} {g : P.Metric}<br>    {F : GC.Interface.RawSurgery P g} {K : ℕ} {cores : PersistentHyperbolicCores F K}<br>    (M : PrescribedCuspMeridian cores) (T : ℝ) (hT : M.exterior.start ≤ T)<br>    (t : ℝ) (ht : T ≤ t) : freeLoop (postStage F.observation t).Carrier :=<br>  M.transported t (hT.trans ht)</code> |
| `GC.LongTime.exists_primitive_meridian_of_compressible_seam` | <code>(new declaration)</code> | <code>theorem exists_primitive_meridian_of_compressible_seam<br>    {P : OrientedThreeStage.{u}} {g : P.Metric} {F : GC.Interface.RawSurgery P g}<br>    (K : ℕ) (hK : lateDerivativeOrder ≤ K) (δ : ℝ → ℝ) (hadm : hasAnalyticAdmissibility F δ)<br>    (hdec : ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 &lt; ε → ∃ B : ℝ, ∀ t : ℝ, B &lt; t → δ t &lt; ε)<br>    {slices : ℕ → RegularSlice F.observation} (L : LateCutFamily F K slices)<br>    (j : ℕ) (hj : L.first ≤ j) (C : ConnectedComponents (slices j).stage.Carrier)<br>    (s : Fin (L.decomposition j C).boundary.count) (x : Torus)<br>    (hcomp : ¬ Function.Injective (FundamentalGroup.map<br>      ((L.decomposition j C).reconstructionAtlas.torusInPrime (L.decomposition j C).reconstruction s) x)) :<br>    Nonempty (PrescribedCuspMeridian L.cores)</code> |
| `GC.LongTime.exists_attained_leastExteriorDiskArea` | <code>(new declaration)</code> | <code>theorem exists_attained_leastExteriorDiskArea<br>    {P : OrientedThreeStage.{u}} {g : P.Metric} {F : GC.Interface.RawSurgery P g}<br>    (K : ℕ) (hK : lateDerivativeOrder ≤ K) (δ : ℝ → ℝ) (hadm : hasAnalyticAdmissibility F δ)<br>    (hdec : ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 &lt; ε → ∃ B : ℝ, ∀ t : ℝ, B &lt; t → δ t &lt; ε)<br>    {cores : PersistentHyperbolicCores F (K + 4)} (M : PrescribedCuspMeridian cores) :<br>    ∃ T₀ : ℝ, ∃ h₀ : M.exterior.start ≤ T₀, ∀ T : ℝ, ∀ h : T₀ ≤ T,<br>      hasExteriorDiskMinimizersAfter F.observation M.exterior.region T (M.loopAfter T (h₀.trans h)) ∧<br>      ∀ t ∈ Ici T, 0 &lt; exteriorDiskArea F.observation M.exterior.region T (M.loopAfter T (h₀.trans h)) t</code> |
| `GC.LongTime.local_disk_comparisons_of_cusp_exterior` | <code>(new declaration)</code> | <code>theorem local_disk_comparisons_of_cusp_exterior<br>    {P : OrientedThreeStage.{u}} {g : P.Metric} {F : GC.Interface.RawSurgery P g}<br>    (K : ℕ) (hK : lateDerivativeOrder ≤ K) (δ : ℝ → ℝ) (hadm : hasAnalyticAdmissibility F δ)<br>    (hdec : ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 &lt; ε → ∃ B : ℝ, ∀ t : ℝ, B &lt; t → δ t &lt; ε)<br>    {cores : PersistentHyperbolicCores F (K + 4)} (M : PrescribedCuspMeridian cores) :<br>    ∃ T₀ : ℝ, ∃ h₀ : M.exterior.start ≤ T₀, ∀ T : ℝ, ∀ h : T₀ ≤ T,<br>      hasExteriorDiskMinimizersAfter F.observation M.exterior.region T (M.loopAfter T (h₀.trans h)) →<br>      hasExteriorDiskComparisonsAfter F.observation M.exterior.region T (M.loopAfter T (h₀.trans h))</code> |
| `GC.LongTime.exists_local_upper_barrier_of_exteriorDiskArea` | <code>(new declaration)</code> | <code>theorem exists_local_upper_barrier_of_exteriorDiskArea<br>    {P : OrientedThreeStage.{u}} {g : P.Metric} {F : GC.Interface.RawSurgery P g}<br>    (K : ℕ) (hK : lateDerivativeOrder ≤ K) (δ : ℝ → ℝ) (H : AnalyticSurgeryProfile F δ)<br>    (hdec : ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 &lt; ε → ∃ B : ℝ, ∀ t : ℝ, B &lt; t → δ t &lt; ε)<br>    {cores : PersistentHyperbolicCores F (K + 4)} (M : PrescribedCuspMeridian cores) :<br>    ∃ T₀ : ℝ, ∃ h₀ : M.exterior.start ≤ T₀, ∀ T : ℝ, ∀ h : T₀ ≤ T,<br>      hasExteriorDiskMinimizersAfter F.observation M.exterior.region T (M.loopAfter T (h₀.trans h)) →<br>      ∀ t ∈ Ici T,<br>        hasLocalSmoothUpperBarrier<br>          (exteriorDiskArea F.observation M.exterior.region T (M.loopAfter T (h₀.trans h))) (Ici T) t<br>          (3 * exteriorDiskArea F.observation M.exterior.region T (M.loopAfter T (h₀.trans h)) t /<br>            (4 * (t + H.scalarShift)) - Real.pi)</code> |
| `GC.LongTime.hasExteriorDiskComparisonsAfter` | <code>(new declaration)</code> | <code>def hasExteriorDiskComparisonsAfter {P : OrientedThreeStage.{u}} {g : P.Metric}<br>    (O : ObservationTower P g) (W : (t : ℝ) → Set (postStage O t).Carrier) (T : ℝ)<br>    (γ : (t : ℝ) → T ≤ t → freeLoop (postStage O t).Carrier) : Prop :=<br>  ∀ (t₀ : ℝ) (ht₀ : T ≤ t₀), ∀ ε &gt; (0 : ℝ), ∃ δ &gt; (0 : ℝ),<br>    ∀ (t : ℝ) (ht : T ≤ t), &#124;t - t₀&#124; &lt; δ →<br>      (∀ u : C(closedDisk, (postStage O t₀).Carrier), isExteriorSpanningDisk (W t₀) (γ t₀ ht₀) u →<br>        Geometry.riemannianDiskArea (postMetric O t₀) u = exteriorDiskArea O W T γ t₀ →<br>        ∃ v : C(closedDisk, (postStage O t).Carrier), isExteriorSpanningDisk (W t) (γ t ht) v ∧<br>          Geometry.riemannianDiskArea (postMetric O t) v ≤ Real.exp ε * Geometry.riemannianDiskArea (postMetric O t₀) u) ∧<br>      (∀ v : C(closedDisk, (postStage O t).Carrier), isExteriorSpanningDisk (W t) (γ t ht) v →<br>        Geometry.riemannianDiskArea (postMetric O t) v = exteriorDiskArea O W T γ t →<br>        ∃ u : C(closedDisk, (postStage O t₀).Carrier), isExteriorSpanningDisk (W t₀) (γ t₀ ht₀) u ∧<br>          Geometry.riemannianDiskArea (postMetric O t₀) u ≤ Real.exp ε * Geometry.riemannianDiskArea (postMetric O t) v)</code> |
| `GC.LongTime.continuousOn_exteriorDiskArea_of_local_comparisons` | <code>(new declaration)</code> | <code>theorem continuousOn_exteriorDiskArea_of_local_comparisons<br>    {P : OrientedThreeStage.{u}} {g : P.Metric}<br>    (O : ObservationTower P g) (W : (t : ℝ) → Set (postStage O t).Carrier) (T : ℝ)<br>    (γ : (t : ℝ) → T ≤ t → freeLoop (postStage O t).Carrier)<br>    (hmin : hasExteriorDiskMinimizersAfter O W T γ)<br>    (hcomp : hasExteriorDiskComparisonsAfter O W T γ) :<br>    ContinuousOn (exteriorDiskArea O W T γ) (Ici T)</code> |
| `GC.LongTime.hasExteriorDiskMinimizersAfter` | <code>(new declaration)</code> | <code>def hasExteriorDiskMinimizersAfter (O : ObservationTower P g)<br>    (W : (t : ℝ) → Set (postStage O t).Carrier) (T : ℝ)<br>    (γ : (t : ℝ) → T ≤ t → freeLoop (postStage O t).Carrier) : Prop :=<br>  ∀ (t : ℝ) (ht : T ≤ t), ∃ u : C(closedDisk, (postStage O t).Carrier),<br>    isExteriorSpanningDisk (W t) (γ t ht) u ∧<br>    DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.riemannianDiskArea (postMetric O t) u =<br>      exteriorDiskArea O W T γ t</code> |
| `GC.LongTime.hasThinVolumeGeometry` | <code>(new declaration)</code> | <code>def hasThinVolumeGeometry (W : CompactCarrier.{u})<br>    (g : SmoothRiemannianMetric W.model W.Carrier) (K : ℕ) (w : ℝ) : Prop :=<br>  (W.model.boundary W.Carrier = ∅ ∧ ∀ p, volumeCollapsedAtCurvatureScale g w p) ∨<br>    (Nonempty (NearlyCuspidalBoundary W g (K + 4) w) ∧ boundaryVolumeCollapsed W g w)</code> |
| `GC.LongTime.HyperbolicOrThin` | <code>(new declaration)</code> | <code>inductive HyperbolicOrThin {M : ConnectedClosedOrientedManifold.{u} 3}<br>    (D : TorusDecomposition M)<br>    (metric : (i : Fin D.components.count) → SmoothRiemannianMetric (D.component i).model (D.component i).Carrier)<br>    (thin : Fin D.components.count → Prop) (K : ℕ) (w : ℝ) (i : Fin D.components.count)<br>  &#124; hyperbolic (not_thin : ¬ thin i) (geometry : D.carrier.InteriorGeometry (D.components.piece i))<br>      (model_eq : isHyperbolicInteriorGeometry geometry)<br>  &#124; thin (member : thin i) (geometry : hasThinVolumeGeometry (D.component i) (metric i) K w)<br>  &#124; nonnegative (not_thin : ¬ thin i) (closed : (D.component i).model.boundary (D.component i).Carrier = ∅)<br>      (curvature : DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Riemannian.SectionalBoundedBelow (metric i) 0)</code> |
| `GC.LongTime.LateCutFamily` | <code>(new declaration)</code> | <code>structure LateCutFamily {P : OrientedThreeStage.{u}} {g : P.Metric}<br>    (F : GC.Interface.RawSurgery P g) (K : ℕ) (slices : ℕ → RegularSlice F.observation) where<br>  cores : PersistentHyperbolicCores F (K + 4)<br>  decomposition : ∀ j (C : ConnectedComponents (slices j).stage.Carrier),<br>    TorusDecomposition ((slices j).stage.toClosedOrientedManifold.component C)<br>  metric : ∀ j C (i : Fin (decomposition j C).components.count),<br>    SmoothRiemannianMetric ((decomposition j C).component i).model ((decomposition j C).component i).Carrier<br>  induced : ∀ j C i, isInducedCutMetric ((slices j).componentMetric C) (decomposition j C) i (metric j C i)<br>  thin : ∀ j C, Fin (decomposition j C).components.count → Prop<br>  first : ℕ<br>  time_late : ∀ j, first ≤ j → cores.start ≤ (slices j).time<br>  truncation : ∀ (_j : ℕ) (i : Fin cores.count), HyperbolicTruncation (cores.model i)<br>  port : ∀ j, first ≤ j →<br>    (Σ C : ConnectedComponents (slices j).stage.Carrier, Fin (decomposition j C).boundary.count) ≃<br>      Σ i : Fin cores.count, Fin (truncation j i).count<br>  core_in_ball : ∀ j, first ≤ j → ∀ i,<br>    range (truncation j i).inclusion ⊆ riemannianBallOf (cores.model i).metric<br>      (cores.model i).basepoint (cores.accuracy (slices j).time)⁻¹<br>  seam_image : ∀ j (hj : first ≤ j) C (s : Fin (decomposition j C).boundary.count),<br>    let p := port j hj ⟨C, s⟩;<br>    HEq (range (fun x : Torus =&gt; cores.map p.1 (slices j).time (time_late j hj)<br>      ((truncation j p.1).cuspMap p.2 (x, halfZero))))<br>      (range (fun x : Torus =&gt; ((decomposition j C).reconstructionAtlas.torusInPrime<br>        (decomposition j C).reconstruction s x).val))<br>  finite_scales : ∀ j C i, thin j C i → ∀ p, curvatureRadius (metric j C i) p ≠ ⊤<br>  alternatives : ∀ w : ℝ, 0 &lt; w → w &lt; euclideanThreeUnitBallVolume →<br>    ∃ N : ℕ, ∀ j, N ≤ j → ∀ C,<br>      Nonempty ((i : Fin (decomposition j C).components.count) →<br>        HyperbolicOrThin (decomposition j C) (metric j C) (thin j C) K w i)<br><br>namespace LateCutFamily<br>variable {P : OrientedThreeStage.{u}} {g : P.Metric}<br>  {F : GC.Interface.RawSurgery P g} {K : ℕ} {slices : ℕ → RegularSlice F.observation}</code> |
| `GC.LongTime.LateCutFamily.thinIndex` | <code>(new declaration)</code> | <code>def thinIndex (L : LateCutFamily F K slices) (j : ℕ) :=<br>  Σ C : ConnectedComponents (slices j).stage.Carrier,<br>    {i : Fin (L.decomposition j C).components.count // L.thin j C i}<br><br>instance (L : LateCutFamily F K slices) (j : ℕ) : Finite (L.thinIndex j) := by<br>  have : LocallyConnectedSpace (slices j).stage.Carrier :=<br>    ChartedSpace.locallyConnectedSpace ThreeSpace (slices j).stage.Carrier<br>  unfold thinIndex<br>  infer_instance</code> |
| `GC.LongTime.LateCutFamily.hasEventualDerivativeBounds` | <code>(new declaration)</code> | <code>def hasEventualDerivativeBounds (L : LateCutFamily F K slices) : Prop :=<br>  ∀ w : ℝ, 0 &lt; w → w &lt; euclideanThreeUnitBallVolume →<br>    ∃ N : ℕ, ∃ C : ℝ, 0 &lt; C ∧<br>      ∀ j, N ≤ j → ∀ c i, L.thin j c i →<br>      ∀ (p : ((L.decomposition j c).component i).Carrier) (r : ℝ), 0 &lt; r →<br>        ENNReal.ofReal r &lt; curvatureRadius (L.metric j c i) p →<br>        ENNReal.ofReal (w * r ^ 3) ≤ ballVolume (L.metric j c i) p r →<br>        ∀ k : ℕ, k ≤ K → ∀ q ∈ riemannianBallOf (L.metric j c i) p r,<br>          curvatureDerivativeNorm (L.metric j c i) k q ≤ C * (r ^ (k + 2))⁻¹</code> |
| `GC.LongTime.LateCutFamily.exists_common_control` | <code>(new declaration)</code> | <code>theorem exists_common_control (L : LateCutFamily F K slices) (h : L.hasEventualDerivativeBounds) :<br>    ∃ A : ℝ → ℝ, (∀ w, 0 &lt; w → 0 &lt; A w) ∧<br>      ∀ j C i, L.thin j C i → ∀ w, 0 &lt; w → curvatureDerivativesControlled (L.metric j C i) K A w</code> |
| `GC.LongTime.hasThinVolumeGeometry.collapseHypotheses` | <code>(new declaration)</code> | <code>theorem hasThinVolumeGeometry.collapseHypotheses {W : CompactCarrier.{u}}<br>    {g : SmoothRiemannianMetric W.model W.Carrier} {K : ℕ} {w : ℝ} {A : ℝ → ℝ}<br>    (h : hasThinVolumeGeometry W g K w) (hcontrol : curvatureDerivativesControlled g K A w) :<br>    staticCollapseHypotheses W g K A w</code> |
| `GC.LongTime.HyperbolicOrThin.toHyperbolicOrCollapsed` | <code>(new declaration)</code> | <code>def HyperbolicOrThin.toHyperbolicOrCollapsed {M : ConnectedClosedOrientedManifold.{u} 3}<br>    {D : TorusDecomposition M}<br>    {metric : (i : Fin D.components.count) → SmoothRiemannianMetric (D.component i).model (D.component i).Carrier}<br>    {thin : Fin D.components.count → Prop} {K : ℕ} {w : ℝ} {i : Fin D.components.count}<br>    (h : HyperbolicOrThin D metric thin K w i)<br>    (g : SmoothRiemannianMetric (𝓡 3) M.Carrier) (A : ℝ → ℝ)<br>    (hinduced : isInducedCutMetric g D i (metric i))<br>    (hcontrol : thin i → curvatureDerivativesControlled (metric i) K A w) :<br>    HyperbolicOrCollapsed g D K A w i := by<br>  cases h with<br>  &#124; hyperbolic _ geometry model_eq =&gt; exact .hyperbolic geometry model_eq<br>  &#124; thin member geometry =&gt; exact .collapsed (metric i) hinduced (geometry.collapseHypotheses (hcontrol member))<br>  &#124; nonnegative _ closed curvature =&gt; exact .nonnegative (metric i) hinduced closed curvature</code> |
| `GC.LongTime.LateCutFamily.exists_late_tests_of_derivative_bounds` | <code>(new declaration)</code> | <code>theorem LateCutFamily.exists_late_tests_of_derivative_bounds<br>    {P : OrientedThreeStage.{u}} {g : P.Metric} {F : GC.Interface.RawSurgery P g}<br>    {K : ℕ} {slices : ℕ → RegularSlice F.observation}<br>    (L : LateCutFamily F K slices) (h : L.hasEventualDerivativeBounds) :<br>    ∃ A : ℝ → ℝ, (∀ w, 0 &lt; w → w &lt; euclideanThreeUnitBallVolume → 0 &lt; A w) ∧<br>      ∀ w, 0 &lt; w → w &lt; euclideanThreeUnitBallVolume → ∃ N : ℕ, ∀ j, N ≤ j → ∀ C,<br>        Nonempty ((i : Fin (L.decomposition j C).components.count) →<br>          HyperbolicOrCollapsed ((slices j).componentMetric C) (L.decomposition j C) K A w i)</code> |
| `GC.LongTime.exists_late_cut_family` | <code>(new declaration)</code> | <code>theorem exists_late_cut_family {P : OrientedThreeStage.{u}} {g : P.Metric}<br>    (F : GC.Interface.RawSurgery P g) (K : ℕ) (hK : lateDerivativeOrder ≤ K) (δ : ℝ → ℝ)<br>    (hadm : hasAnalyticAdmissibility F δ)<br>    (hdec : ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 &lt; ε → ∃ B : ℝ, ∀ t : ℝ, B &lt; t → δ t &lt; ε)<br>    (slices : ℕ → RegularSlice F.observation)<br>    (htimes : ∀ j : ℕ, (j : ℝ) &lt; (slices j).time)<br>    (hnonempty : ∀ j : ℕ, Nonempty (slices j).stage.Carrier) :<br>    Nonempty (LateCutFamily F K slices)</code> |

## Retired APIs

These were unused wrappers or redundant variants explicitly covered by Tasks1,3.4 and6.8. Their consumers were not deleted to remove admissions; no direct admission was removed by deleting its consumer.
